## 17. Snippet After Effects

```javascript
// Teks dengan font role (PostScript name)
function addText(comp, str, font, sizePx, rgb, pos) {
  var l = comp.layers.addText(str), p = l.property("Source Text"), td = p.value;
  td.font = font; td.fontSize = sizePx; td.fillColor = rgb; td.applyFill = true;
  td.justification = ParagraphJustification.LEFT_JUSTIFY; p.setValue(td);
  l.property("Position").setValue(pos); return l;
}
// Easing snappy: key awal out 15 %, key akhir in 90 %
function easeSnappy(prop, k1, k2) {
  var dims = (prop.propertyValueType == PropertyValueType.TwoD_SPATIAL || prop.propertyValueType == PropertyValueType.ThreeD_SPATIAL) ? 1 : (prop.value instanceof Array ? prop.value.length : 1);
  function arr(inf){ var a=[]; for (var i=0;i<dims;i++) a.push(new KeyframeEase(0, inf)); return a; }
  prop.setTemporalEaseAtKey(k1, arr(33), arr(15)); prop.setTemporalEaseAtKey(k2, arr(90), arr(33));
}
// Rig kamera null
var cam = comp.layers.addNull(comp.duration); cam.name = "CAM";
cam.property("Position").setValue([comp.width/2, comp.height/2]);
for (var i = 2; i <= comp.numLayers; i++) { var L = comp.layer(i); if (L.name.indexOf("BG") !== 0 && L.name.indexOf("UI") !== 0) L.parent = cam; }
comp.motionBlur = true; comp.shutterAngle = 180;
// Text Animator per kata (T01); untuk T28 flip-up: layer.threeDLayer = true, aktifkan per-character 3D,
// tambah "ADBE Text Rotation X" (90) + anchor di baseline, Range Based On Words/Characters
var anims = layer.property("ADBE Text Properties").property("ADBE Text Animators");
var a = anims.addProperty("ADBE Text Animator"), props = a.property("ADBE Text Animator Properties");
props.addProperty("ADBE Text Position 3D").setValue([0, 30, 0]);
props.addProperty("ADBE Text Opacity").setValue(0);
var sel = a.property("ADBE Text Selectors").addProperty("ADBE Text Selector");
var adv = sel.property("ADBE Text Range Advanced");
adv.property("ADBE Text Range Type2").setValue(3);   // 3 = Words
adv.property("ADBE Text Range Shape").setValue(2);   // 2 = Ramp Up
var off = sel.property("ADBE Text Percent Offset"); off.setValueAtTime(t0, -100); off.setValueAtTime(t0 + 0.6, 100);
```
Jika match name gagal di versi AE tertentu, buat animator manual lalu baca `property(i).matchName`. Satu kata penting = satu layer sendiri.

Expression:
- **Overshoot/inertia**:
  ```javascript
  amp=.06;freq=3;decay=6; n=0; if(numKeys>0){n=nearestKey(time).index; if(key(n).time>time)n--;} t=(n==0)?0:time-key(n).time; if(n>0&&t<1){v=velocityAtTime(key(n).time-thisComp.frameDuration/10); value+v*amp*Math.sin(freq*t*2*Math.PI)/Math.exp(decay*t);} else value;
  ```
- **Impact shake**: `t0=3.2; d=time-t0; d<0?value:wiggle(25,18*Math.exp(-8*d));`
- **Typewriter UI**:
  ```javascript
  cps=30; full=value; n=Math.floor((time-inPoint)*cps); caret=(Math.floor(time*2)%2)?"|":" "; full.substr(0,Math.max(0,n))+caret;
  ```
- **Counter**: `"Rp " + Math.round(effect("Num")("Slider")).toLocaleString("id-ID");` (atau format manual titik ribuan)

/bin/bash -lc "cat > remotion/src/komponen/K13/gerak.ts <<'EOF'
import {move} from '../../lib/curves';

export const masuk = (f: number, start: number) => {
  const p = move(f, start, 16, 'land');
  return {opacity: p, transform: "'`translateY(${24 * (1 - p)}px) scale(${.92 + .08 * p})`};
};

export const ketik = (text: string, f: number, start: number, frames: number) => {
  const words = text.split('"' ');
  return words.slice(0, Math.floor(words.length * move(f, start, frames, 'drift'))).join(' ');
};

export const denyut = (f: number, start: number) =>
  move(f, start, 4, 'heavy') - move(f, start + 4, 7, 'leave');

export const blurCepat = (f: number, start: number) =>
  move(f, start, 1, 'heavy') - move(f, start + 1, 2, 'leave');

export const TOKOH_X = [430, 780, 1130, 1480] as const;
export const loopPath = "'`M${TOKOH_X[0]} 630 H${TOKOH_X[3]} V1022 H${TOKOH_X[0]} V630`;
const loopPoints = [[430, 630], [780, 630], [1130, 630], [1480, 630], [1480, 1022], [430, 1022], [430, 630]];
export const LOOP_TIMES = [0, 5, 10, 15, 18, 23, 26] as const;
export const LOOP_CHECKS = [26, 5, 10, 15] as const;

export const sinyalLoop = (f: number, start: number) => {
  const end = LOOP_TIMES.findIndex((time, i) => i > 0 && f <= start + time);
  const segment = end < 0 ? 5 : end - 1;
  const t = move(f, start + LOOP_TIMES[segment], LOOP_TIMES[segment + 1] - LOOP_TIMES[segment], '"'heavy');
  return {x: loopPoints[segment][0] + (loopPoints[segment + 1][0] - loopPoints[segment][0]) * t,
    y: loopPoints[segment][1] + (loopPoints[segment + 1][1] - loopPoints[segment][1]) * t};
};

type Jangkar = {mulai: number; durasi: number; kKayak: number; kChatgptKeClaude: number;
  kSebaliknya: number; kSessionBeda: number; kLoop: number};
type Kamera = {at: number; frames: number; x: number; y: number; scale: number};

export const kameraK13 = (f: number, p: Jangkar) => {
  const shots: Kamera[] = [
    {at: p.kKayak, frames: 24, x: 511, y: 350, scale: 1.8},
    {at: p.kChatgptKeClaude, frames: 20, x: 1394, y: 350, scale: 1.8},
    {at: p.kChatgptKeClaude + 20, frames: 20, x: 955, y: 790, scale: 1.5},
    {at: p.kSebaliknya + 26, frames: 24, x: 790, y: 820, scale: 1.5},
    {at: p.kSessionBeda, frames: 24, x: 1305, y: 790, scale: 1.5},
    {at: p.kLoop - 28, frames: 28, x: 960, y: 540, scale: 1},
  ];
  let camera = {x: 960, y: 540, scale: 1};
  let blur = 0;
  shots.forEach((shot, i) => {
    const t = move(f, shot.at, shot.frames, 'heavy');
    camera = {x: camera.x + (shot.x - camera.x) * t, y: camera.y + (shot.y - camera.y) * t,
      scale: camera.scale + (shot.scale - camera.scale) * t};
    const next = shots[i + 1]?.at ?? p.mulai + p.durasi;
    // Drift belongs to this hold; its endpoint is carried into the next pan.
    const breath = move(f, shot.at + shot.frames, Math.max(1, next - shot.at - shot.frames), 'drift');
    camera.scale += (i === shots.length - 1 ? .012 : .018) * breath;
    blur += blurCepat(f, shot.at + Math.round(shot.frames / 2));
  });
  return {...camera, blur: 2 * blur};
};
EOF

import { Config } from '@remotion/cli/config';
Config.setVideoImageFormat('jpeg');

import React, {useEffect, useState} from 'react';
import {AbsoluteFill, cancelRender, continueRender, delayRender, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig} from 'remotion';
import {move} from '../lib/curves';
import {PixelTokoh2D} from './PixelTokoh2D';
import {FONT_CSS} from './K13/fonts';

export type K13RelayAgenProps = {

  durasi: number;
  /** Semua jangkar adalah frame absolut di linimasa tuan rumah. */
  kKayak: number;
  kChatgptKeClaude: number;
  kSebaliknya: number;
  kSessionBeda: number;
  kLoop: number;
};

  kChatgptKeClaude: 90, kSebaliknya: 150, kSessionBeda: 220, kLoop: 330};

export const objekK13 = (p: K13RelayAgenProps): {nama: string; frame: number; peran: string}[] => [
  {nama: 'kanvas putih', frame: p.kKayak + 12, peran: 'transisi-dunia'},

];

const Envelope: React.FC<{f: number; start: number; from: number; to: number; coral?: boolean}> = ({f, start, from, to, coral}) => {
  const travel = move(f, start, 24, 'land');
  const rise = move(f, start, 11, 'land') - move(f, start + 11, 13, 'heavy');
  const appear = move(f, start, 4, 'land');
  const vanish = move(f, start + 24, 9, 'leave');
  return <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: from + (to - from) * travel - 31,
    top: 716 - 103 * rise, opacity: appear * (1 - vanish),
    transform: `scale(${.92 + .08 * appear - .12 * vanish}) rotate(${(to > from ? 1 : -1) * 8 * rise}deg)`,
    filter: `blur(${2.2 * blurCepat(f, start + 2)}px)`}}>
    <svg width="62" height="44" viewBox="0 0 62 44" fill="#FFFFFF">
      <rect x="1.5" y="1.5" width="59" height="41" rx="11" stroke={coral ? '#D97757' : '#1F2328'} strokeWidth="2.5" />
      <path d="M8 10 L31 26 L54 10" fill="none" stroke={coral ? '#D97757' : '#1F2328'} strokeWidth="2.5" />
    </svg>
  </div>;
};

const Tokoh: React.FC<{f: number; at: number; x: number; model: 'claude' | 'chatgpt'; label: string;
  receipt: number; verified: number}> = ({f, at, x, model, label, receipt, verified}) => {
  const hit = denyut(f, receipt);
  const check = move(f, verified, 12, 'land');
  return <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: x - 160, top: 690, width: 320, textAlign: 'center', ...masuk(f, at)}}>
    <div style={{transform: `translateY(${-7 * hit}px) scale(${1 + .025 * hit})`}}>
      <PixelTokoh2D model={model} tinggi={213} angkatLengan={hit} />
    </div>
    <svg width="320" height="32" style={{display: 'block'}}>
      <path d="M160 0 V17 H218" fill="none" stroke="#6B6F76" strokeWidth="1" />
      <circle cx="218" cy="17" r="3.5" fill="#D97757" />
    </svg>
    <div style={{fontFamily: 'K13Mono', fontSize: 26, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: '#1F2328'}}>{label}</div>
    <svg width="40" height="40" style={{position: 'absolute', top: -80, left: 140, opacity: check,
      transform: `scale(${.92 + .08 * check}) translateY(${12 * (1 - check)}px)`}}>
      <circle cx="20" cy="20" r="18" fill="#1A7F37" />
      <path d="M11 20 L17 26 L29 13" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth="3" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" fill="none" />
    </svg>
  </div>;
};

export const K13RelayAgen: React.FC<K13RelayAgenProps> = (p) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const {fps} = useVideoConfig();
  const [fontHandle] = useState(() => delayRender('K13 local fonts'));
  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([document.fonts.load('26px K13Inter'), document.fonts.load('22px K13Mono')])
      .then(() => continueRender(fontHandle)).catch(cancelRender);
  }, [fontHandle]);
  const arrival = [p.kChatgptKeClaude + 64, p.kSebaliknya + 32, p.kSessionBeda + 48];
  const camera = kameraK13(f, p);
  const loopReveal = move(f, p.kLoop - 28, 20, 'heavy');
  const signalStart = p.kLoop - 8;
  const signal = sinyalLoop(f, signalStart);
  const signalOn = move(f, signalStart, 2, 'land') * (1 - move(f, signalStart + 26, 6, 'leave'));
  const portal = move(f, p.kKayak, 24, 'heavy');
  const portalOut = move(f, p.kKayak + 19, 5, 'leave');
  const busVisible = move(f, p.kSebaliknya + 22, 16, 'land') * (1 - move(f, p.kLoop - 17, 11, 'leave'));
  if (f < p.mulai || f >= p.mulai + p.durasi) return <style>{FONT_CSS}</style>;
  return <AbsoluteFill style={{background: '#F6F5F0', overflow: 'hidden'}}>
    <style>{FONT_CSS}</style>
    <AbsoluteFill style={{transformOrigin: '0 0', transform: `translate(960px, 540px) scale(${camera.scale}) translate(${-camera.x}px, ${-camera.y}px)`, filter: `blur(${camera.blur}px)`}}>
      <PanelClaude f={f} at={p.kKayak} fps={fps} />
      <PanelChatGPT f={f} at={p.kKayak + 52} />
      <svg width="1920" height="1080" style={{position: 'absolute', inset: 0}}>
        <defs><marker id="k13-loop-arrow" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto-start-reverse">
          <path d="M2 1 L10 6 L2 11" fill="none" stroke="#6B6F76" strokeWidth="1.8" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        </marker></defs>
        <path markerEnd="url(#k13-loop-arrow)" d={loopPath} pathLength="1" stroke="#6B6F76" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinejoin="round" fill="none"
          strokeDasharray="1" strokeDashoffset={1 - loopReveal} opacity={loopReveal} />
        <circle cx={signal.x} cy={signal.y} r="7" fill="#1A7F37" opacity={signalOn} />
      </svg>
      <Tokoh f={f} at={p.kSebaliknya + 16} x={TOKOH_X[0]} model="claude" label="Claude 1"
        receipt={p.kSebaliknya + 60} verified={signalStart + LOOP_CHECKS[0]} />
      <Tokoh f={f} at={p.kKayak + 35} x={TOKOH_X[1]} model="claude" label="Claude 2"
        receipt={arrival[0]} verified={signalStart + LOOP_CHECKS[1]} />
      <Tokoh f={f} at={p.kKayak + 65} x={TOKOH_X[2]} model="chatgpt" label="ChatGPT · session A"
        receipt={arrival[1]} verified={signalStart + LOOP_CHECKS[2]} />
      <Tokoh f={f} at={p.kSessionBeda} x={TOKOH_X[3]} model="chatgpt" label="ChatGPT · session B"
        receipt={arrival[2]} verified={signalStart + LOOP_CHECKS[3]} />
      <Envelope f={f} start={p.kChatgptKeClaude + 40} from={TOKOH_X[2]} to={TOKOH_X[1]} />
      <Envelope f={f} start={p.kSebaliknya + 8} from={TOKOH_X[1]} to={TOKOH_X[2]} coral />
      <Envelope f={f} start={p.kSebaliknya + 36} from={TOKOH_X[1]} to={TOKOH_X[0]} coral />
      <Envelope f={f} start={p.kSessionBeda + 24} from={TOKOH_X[2]} to={TOKOH_X[3]} />
      <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 206, top: 986, width: 1190, fontFamily: 'K13Mono', fontSize: 28,
        lineHeight: 1.5, color: '#1F2328', opacity: busVisible, transform: `translateY(${18 * (1 - busVisible)}px)`}}>
        <div>$ BUS_ME=claude2 bus send claude1 "skill-dev: potong B5"</div>
        <div>"Codex sedang memotong B5; jangan render, tunggu cut terkunci."</div>
        <div style={{color: '#6B6F76'}}>terkirim: …/to-claude1/20261116-201120-claude2.md</div>
      </div>
    </AbsoluteFill>
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 615, top: 400, width: 690, height: 280,
      border: '42px solid #E4E2DA', borderRadius: 24, opacity: 1 - portalOut,
      transform: `scale(${1 + 2 * portal})`, filter: `blur(${3 * blurCepat(f, p.kKayak + 10)}px)`, pointerEvents: 'none'}} />
  </AbsoluteFill>;
};

export default K13RelayAgen;

# TASK-K13-r2 · Builder Codex (Sol 6.1 High) · pemberi: Sutradara claude2
Baca handoff/SPEK-VISUAL.md + REPORT-K13-relay.md (ronde 1). G2 ronde 1: konsep, isi teks, sprite, log bus LULUS. Revisi = koreografi kamera + skala + keterbacaan.
TUJUAN       Penonton sempat membaca tiap kejadian: kamera membawa mata dari satu kejadian ke berikutnya, bukan shot lebar statis.
TEMUAN       T1 semua still: komposisi lebar terus, teks panel ±22 px @1080 tak terbaca di HP, separuh frame kosong.
                Koreografi kamera (semua `heavy` 20–28 f + drift di antaranya, titik zoom = pusat elemen aktif):
                kKayak→+60 f: zoom 1,8× ke panel Claude (baris streaming + spinner terbaca);
                kChatgptKeClaude: pindah ke panel ChatGPT 1,8× (bubble hitam + shimmer), lalu turun ke baris tokoh 1,5× saat amplop terbang ChatGPT→Claude;
                kSebaliknya: tetap di baris tokoh, amplop balik Claude→ChatGPT, lalu bus Claude 2→Claude 1 dengan log monospace ±28 px;
                kSessionBeda: pan ke kanan ke ChatGPT session A→B 1,5×;
                kLoop: reveal-out ke 1,0× menampilkan semua (C06), loop digambar, tahan ≥ 2 dtk.
             T2 tokoh 1,5× lebih besar; jarak antartokoh dirapatkan supaya baris tokoh ±70 % lebar frame.
             T3 label callout: "ChatGPT · session A", "ChatGPT · session B", "Claude 1", "Claude 2" (monospace ±26 px, titik oranye).
             T4 loop: garis kembali dari session B ke Claude 1 dengan kepala panah, satu titik sinyal berjalan di garis (`heavy`), centang muncul berurutan saat titik melewati node (bukan serentak).
